     const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions on a local slab.\n// The local field has one halo plane on each side (planes 0 and lnz+1);\n// zl is the local plane index in [1, lnz], gz the corresponding global z.\n__device__ inline double computeLaplacianDev(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                             const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                             const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t zl,\n                                             const size_t gz) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n + 1;\n        const size_t rem = i % plane;\n        const size_t y = rem / nx;\n        const size_t x = rem % nx;\n        const size_t gz = z_off + zl - 1;\n        const size_t idx = idx3(x, y,                           const size_t nz, const size_t z_off, const size_t lnz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zl = 0; zl < lnz; ++zl) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n                // maxVal = c[0];\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||:bad) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n      const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions on a local slab.\n// The local field has one halo plane on each side (planes 0 and lnz+1);\n// zl is the local plane index in [1, lnz], gz the corresponding global z.\n__device__ inline double computeLaplacianDev(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                             const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                             const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t zl,\n                                             const size_t gz) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    \n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within nz, dx, dy, dz, \n                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        \n        // Update concentration\n        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(cold, cnew);\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = (planes 0 and lnz+1);\n// zl is the local plane index in [1, lnz], gz the corresponding global z.\n__device__ inline double computeLaplacianDev(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                             const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                             const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t zl,\n                                             const size_t gz) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y                                 const size_t lnz, const size_t z_off,\n                                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n double* h_recv_hi) {\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n\n    if (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send_lo, d_field + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf, and minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * computeLaplacianDev(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, gz);\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step on the local slab (halo planes of mu must be current)\n__global__ void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                         const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                         const size_t lnz, const size_t z_off,\n                                         const double D, const double dt,\n                                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t n = plane * lnz;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const size_t zl = i / plane + 1;\n        const size_t rem = i % plane;\n        const size_t y = rem / nx;\n        const size_t x = rem % nx;\n        const size_t gz = z_off + zl - 1;\n        const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D *\n                   computeLaplacianDev(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, gz);\n    }\n}\n\n// Exchange halo planes of a device field with the z-neighbors.\n// field points at the local slab including halos; plane = nx*ny doubles.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const size_t plane, const size_t lnz,\n                   const int prev, const int next,\n                   double* h_send_lo, double* h_recv_lo,\n                   double* h_send_hi, double* h_recv_hi) {\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n\n    if (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send_lo, d_field + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send_hi, d_field + lnz * plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    MPI_Sendrecv(h_send_lo, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, prev, 0,\n                 h_recv_hi, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n    MPI_Sendrecv(h_send_hi, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 1,\n                 h_recv_lo, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, prev, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n\n    if (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_field, h_recv_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_field + (lnz + 1) * plane, h_recv_hi, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (OpenMP on the host)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                             const size_t nz, const size_t z_off, const size_t lnz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zl = 0; zl < lnz; ++zl) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (z_off + zl) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf, and compute range (OpenMP on the host)\n    bool bad = false;\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||:bad) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: device-to-host stage (overlapped with interior computation on another\n// stream) and a finish stage doing MPI transfer plus host-to-device copy.\n// field points at the local slab including halos; plane = nx*ny doubles.\nstruct HaloExchange {\n    size_t plane = 0;\n    size_t lnz = 0;\n    int prev = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    int next = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    double* h_send_lo = nullptr;\n    double* void startD2H(const double* d_field) const {\n        const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n        if (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_lo, d_field + boundary conditions on a local slab.\n// The local field has one halo plane on each side (planes 0 and lnz+1);\n// zl is the local plane index in [1, lnz], gz the corresponding global z.\n__device__ inline double computeLaplacianDev(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                             const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                             const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t zl,\n \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                   \\\n    do {                                                                                   \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                         \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_),          \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                                   \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                  \\\n        }                                                                                  \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,\n                                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions on a local slab.\n// The local field has one halo plane on each side (planes 0 and lnz+1);\n// zl is the local plane index in [1, lnz], gz the corresponding global z.\n__device__ inline double computeLaplacianDev(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                             const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                             const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t zl,\n                                             const size_t gz) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (gz < nz - 1) ? zl + 1 : zl;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (gz > 0) ? zl - 1 : zl;\n\n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, zl, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, zl, nx, ny)] -\n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, zl, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, zl, nx, ny)] -\n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] -\n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential on local planes [zl_start, zl_start + nplanes)\n// (halo planes of c must be current if the range touches them)\n__global__ void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                               const size_t zl_start, const size_t nplanes,\n                                               const size_t z_off,\n                                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                               const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t n = plane * nplanes;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const size_t zl = i / plane + zl_start;\n        const size_t rem = i % plane;\n        const size_t y = rem / nx;\n e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * computeLaplacianDev(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, gz);\n    }\n}\n\n// nplanes;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const size_t zl = i / plane + zl_start;\n        const cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D *\n                   computeLaplacianDev(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, gz);\n    }\n}\n\n// Halo exchange of one plane per z-neighbor, split into an asynchronous\n// device-to-host stage (overlapped with interior computation on another\n// stream) and a finish stage doing MPI transfer plus host-to-device copy.\n// field points at the local slab including halos; plane = nx*ny doubles.\nstruct HaloExchange {\n    size_t plane = 0;\n    size_t lnz = 0;\n    int prev = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    int next = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    double* h_send_lo = nullptr;\n    double* h_recv_lo = nullptr;\n    double* h_send_hi = nullptr;\n    double* h_recv_hi = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n\n    // Stage boundary planes to the host (async on the copy stream)\n    void startD2H(const double* d_field) const {\n        const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n        if (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_lo, d_field + plane, bytes,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_hi, d_field + lnz * plane, bytes,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n    }\n\n    // Complete the MPI exchange and load received planes into the halos\n    void finish(double* d_field) const {\n        if (prev == MPI_PROC_NULL && next == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n        const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        MPI_Sendrecv(h_send_lo, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, prev, 0,\n                     h_recv_hi, (int)plane, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||:bad) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_hi, d_field + lnz * plane, bytes,\n }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf, and CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_send_hi, plane * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recv_hi, plane * sizeof(double)));\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    if (rank == 0) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&copyStream));\n    cudaEvent_t boundaryDone, stepDone;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&boundaryDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&stepDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n    HaloExchange halo{plane, lnz, prev, next, h_send_lo, h_recv_lo, h_send_hi, h_recv_hi, copyStream};\n\n    // Planes whose stencil reads a halo are deferred until the exchange\n    // completes; everything else (\"interior\") overlaps with communication.\n    const size_t loDefer = (prev != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t hiDefer = (next != MPI_PROC_NULL && lnz > loDefer) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t intStart = 1 + loDefer;\n    const size_t intPlanes = lnz - loDefer - hiDefer;\n\n    const int blockSize = 256;\n    auto blocksFor = [&](size_t nplanes) {\n        const size_t n = (plane * nplanes + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;\n        return (int)std::min<size_t>(std::max<size_t>(n, 1), 65535);\n    };\n\n    auto launchMu = [&](size_t zl_start, size_t nplanes) {\n        if (nplanes == 0) return;\n        computeChemicalPotentialKernel<<<blocksFor(nplanes), blockSize, 0, computeStream>>>(\n            d_cold, d_mu, nx, ny, nz, zl_start, nplanes, z_off, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n    auto launchUpdate = [&](size_t zl_start, size_t nplanes) {\n        if (nplanes == 0) return;\n        cahnHilliardUpdateKernel<<<blocksFor(nplanes), blockSize, 0, computeStream>>>(\n            d_cnew, d_cold, d_mu, nx, ny, nz, zl_start, nplanes, z_off, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n\n    // Run simulation\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        // Stage concentration boundary planes while computing interior mu\n        halo.startD2H(d_cold);\n        launchMu(intStart, intPlanes);\n        halo.finish(d_cold);\n\n        // Compute mu on the halo-dependent boundary planes\n        launchMu(1, loDefer);\n        if (lnz > loDefer) launchMu(lnz, hiDefer);\n\n        // Stage mu boundary planes (after they are computed) while\n        // updating the interior concentration\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(boundaryDone, computeStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(copyStream, boundaryDone, 0));\n        halo.startD2H(d_mu);\n        launchUpdate(intStart, intPlanes);\n        halo.finish(d_mu);\n\n        // Update the halo-dependent boundary planes\n        launchUpdate(1, loDefer);\n        if (lnz > loDefer) launchUpdate(lnz, hiDefer);\n\n        // Next step's staging must see this step's boundary results\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stepDone, computeStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(copyStream, stepDone, 0));\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    // Copy the local slab back and gather the full field on rank 0\n    if (lnz > 0)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostSlab.data(), d_cold + plane, localSize * sizeof(double),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    std::vector<double> cold;\n    std::vector<int> counts(size), displs(size);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const size_t rlnz = base + ((size_t)r < extra ? 1 : 0);\n        const size_t roff = (size_t)r * base + std::min((size_t)r, extra);\n        counts[r] = (int)(rlnz * plane);\n        displs[r] = (int)(roff * plane);\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) cold.resize(gridSize);\n    MPI_Gatherv(hostSlab.data(), (int)localSize, MPI_DOUBLE,\n                rank == 0 ? cold.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance\n        double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send_lo));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv_lo));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send_hi));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv_hi));\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":451,"oldLines":6,"newStart":451,"newLines":10,"lines":["     }","     MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);"," ","+ {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CemcS84VXEk4HBsMYwJ9L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program with near-linear multi-GPU scaling and bit-identical results to the original algorithm.\n\n## What was changed\n\n**`cahn_hilliard.cpp`** (same file, now compiled as CUDA; executable name unchanged):\n- **MPI**: 1D slab decomposition along z. Each rank owns a contiguous block of z-planes with one halo plane per side, exchanged via `MPI_Sendrecv` through pinned host staging buffers. Ranks bind to GPUs round-robin by node-local rank (via `MPI_Comm_split_type`), so it works across multiple nodes of an accelerator cluster. The final field is assembled on rank 0 with `MPI_Gatherv` for the unchanged output/validation paths.\n- **CUDA**: both stencil phases (chemical potential and concentration update) run as GPU kernels with grid-stride loops; the fields stay resident on the device for all timesteps — only single halo planes cross PCIe. Communication is fully overlapped with computation: interior planes compute on one stream while boundary planes are staged, exchanged over MPI, and loaded back on a copy stream (ordered with CUDA events); the halo-dependent boundary planes compute afterward. The clamped (Neumann-style) boundary handling uses global z indices, so decomposed results match the single-domain code exactly.\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes host-side field initialization and the validation min/max/NaN scan.\n- Edge cases handled: works launched directly without `mpirun`, with more ranks than z-planes (idle ranks), and with non-cubic grids.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`**: enables the CUDA language (source-property override keeps the `.cpp` filename), finds MPI/OpenMP/CUDAToolkit — all preinstalled, no new external dependencies — and compiles with `-O3` plus `--fmad=false` so GPU arithmetic matches the CPU expression order.\n\n## Verification\n- Output hashes are **bit-identical** to the original algorithm (compiled without FMA contraction) and **invariant across 1–4 ranks**, for 64³, 33×17×65, and 16×16×2 grids; validation passes.\n- Performance at 384³×50 steps: ~3,200 MCellUpdates/s on 1 GPU → 5,910 (2) → 8,400 (3) → **~11,300 MCellUpdates/s on 4 GPUs** (~3.6× scaling despite PCIe-gen1 links, thanks to the compute/communication overlap; the original serial code measures ~80 MCellUpdates/s, so roughly 140× on 4 GPUs).\n\nOne note: the machine's GPUs sit on very slow PCIe links, which is why the overlap restructuring mattered — before it, 3 ranks were slower than 2; after it, scaling is near-linear."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":516,"cache_read_input_tokens":50619,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":516},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"53a28d11-9fb8-44c2-8aae-67bffdf62f10","uuid":"fd76316e-b045-4547-ba64-bfb93aabafe3","timestamp":"2026-09-06T05:12:03.146Z","request_id":"req_011CemcS6Bdq6mbuGxb8jfeR"}
